		mes "�����? �� ����...";
		mes "���� �� ?";
		mes "���..?";
		close;
	}
	if (ep13_3_invite >= 4) {
		mes "[Entrance Manager]";
		mes "Voce e visiting o capital cidade, El Dicastes?";
		next;
		if(select("Sim.:Nao.") == 2) {
			mes "[Entrance Manager]";
			mes "Entao usar o tunnel proximo tempo.";
			close; 
		}
		mes "[Entrance Manager]";
		mes "Tenhum cuidado.";
		close2;
		warp "dic_dun01",33,212; 
		end; 
	}
	mes "[Entrance Manager]";
	mes "Desculpe.";
	mes "I nao pode let you pass porque you are nao um Sapha.";
	close;
}

dic_dun01,266,113,5	script	Curious Sapha#ep13_3_	449,{
	if (!isequipped(2782)) {
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "����� �� ��?";
		next;
		mes "- Nao pode underste um word. -";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Curious Sapha]";
	mes "Ola?";
	next;
	switch(select("O que sao you fazendo aqui?:Buy research itens.:Ola!:Eu quero to ajudar seu research.")) {
	case 1:
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "Voce ver esse dirty vigilante sobre la?";
		next;
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "He was attacked by Scarabas depois caindo parum dentro Scarabum Hall.";
		mes "E he was o apenas one attacked ate mesmo com others ao redou him.";
		next;
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "Eu acho he slipped e fell on alguns secretion of o Rainhum Scaraba. I pensou erum um accident was procurando parum dentro it.";
		next;
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "Se o research results are correct, o Scarabas are muito sensitive to o smell.";
		mes "Ou else dizer they ir louco sobre it. Almost as se they were perdendo it...";
		next;
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "I came to research o relationship entre o Rainhum secretion component e o Scarabas.";
		mes "Entao I criou este parum dentro um perfume to carregar it easily.";
		next;
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "I pode ser capaz de usar este perfume's reaction to Scarabas e fazer progress in research.";
		close;
	case 2:
		if (checkweight(1201,1) == 0) {
			mes "[Curious Sapha]";
			mes "Voce nao parecer to have suficiente space.";
			close;
		}
		if (MaxWeight - Weight < 2000) {
			mes "[Curious Sapha]";
			mes "Seu bags jum parecer tambem heavy.";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "Voce quer to comprar o Scarabum Perfume? A gift fou home?";
		mes "Bem, it has se tornar um rare perfume.";
		next;
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "Eu vou dar you um deal e vender it by 500 Zeny each. But, tentar nao to carregar it com you parum dentro Scarabum Hall.";
		mes "Quantos voce querer? Voce pode comprar up to 100.";
		next;
		input .@input;
		set .@deal, 500 * .@input;
		if (.@deal == 0) {
			mes "[Curious Sapha]";
			mes "You mudou seu mente?";
			close; 
		}
		if (Zeny < .@deal) {
			mes "[Curious Sapha]";
			mes "Voce nao have suficiente dinheiro.";
			close; 
		}
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "Will you comprar "+.@input+"?";
		next;
		if(select("Sim:Nao") == 2) {
			mes "[Curious Sapha]";
			mes "You mudou seu mente?";
			close;
		}
		set Zeny, Zeny - .@deal;
		getitem 6437,.@input; //Scaraba_Perfume
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "Aqui you ir. Scarabum might flock ao redou you entao nao usar it perto Scarabum Hall.";
		close;
	case 3:
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "Ola? Voce deve be um traveler. Este lugar is perigoentao entao pou favou encontrar seu caminho out of este tunnel.";
		close;
	case 4:
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "Voce have any useful informacao?";
		mes "Pou favou deixe-me saber!";
		input .@str$;
		next;
		mes "[Curious Sapha]";
		mes "It says ^0000FF"+.@str$+"^000000.";
		next;
		switch(rand(1,3)) {
		case 1:
			mes "[Curious Sapha]";
			mes "Esse is otimo informacao!";
			mes "I must comecar on o research.";
			close;
		case 2:
			mes "[Curious Sapha]";
			mes "I jum saber sobre esse informacao mas Eu sou realmente grateful fou you to vir todos o caminho aqui to me diga.";
			close;
		case 3:
			mes "[Curious Sapha]";
			mes "Hmm... voce pensar so?";
			mes "Eu tenho alguns doubts sobre seu informacao. Eu vou pensar it sobre.";
			close;
		}
	}
}

sec_in02,16,43,5	script	Scaraba Dungeon Entrance	449,{
	callfunc "F_GM_NPC";
	mes "Setting you fou masmorrum entry.";
	mes "Voce vai be capaz de ir atraves se o password is correct.";
	if (callfunc("F_GM_NPC",1854,0) == 1) {
		set ep13_3_secret,6;
		getitem 2782,1; //Ring_Of_Wise_King
		getitem 6437,2; //Scaraba_Perfume
		mes "Setting Completar";
	} else
		mes "Errado Password";
	close;
}

		close;
	}

	next;
	mes "[Vigilante]";

	next;

}

		next;

		mes "...Realmente?";

	}
	end;
}

sec_in02,10,42,1	script	13.3 Related Reset	449,{
	callfunc "F_GM_NPC";
	mes "[Reset]";
	mes "Resets todos missao windows including daily e map missoes fou 13.3.";
	freeloop(1);
	for(set .@i,12099; .@i<=12216; set .@i,.@i+1) {
		if (checkquest(.@i) > -1)
			erasequest .@i;
	}
	freeloop(0);
	close;
}

// Civil Services
//============================================================
function	script	que_dic	{
	.@type = getarg(0);
	if (!.@type) {
		if (checkquest(getarg(1),HUNTING) != 2) return;
		mes getarg(3); 
	}
	else if (.@type == 1) {
		if (checkquest(getarg(1)) == -1) return;
		if (countitem(getarg(3)) < getarg(4)) {
			mes "It looks like you are short on o amount.";
			close; 
		}
		mes getarg(5); 
		mes getarg(6,"");
		delitem getarg(3), getarg(4); 
	}
	else if (.@type == 2) {
		for(set .@i,1; .@i<getargcount(); set .@i,.@i+1) {
			if (checkquest(getarg(.@i)) >= 0)
				erasequest getarg(.@i);
		}
		return;
	}
	getexp getarg(2),0;
	erasequest getarg(1);
	specialeffect2 EF_STEAL;
	getitem 6304,1;
	close;
}

dic_in01,48,263,5	script	Hunting Dept. 1 Manager	449,{
	if (checkweight(1201,1) == 0) {
		mes "Voce tem tambem muitos kinds of itens. Vamos tentar novamente depois you reduce o number of itens.";
		close;
	}
	if ((MaxWeight - Weight) < 1000) {
		mes "Nao pode proceed porque o weight of seu itens is tambem heavy. Vamos tentar novamente depois you pegar rid of alguns weight.";
		close;
	}
	if (!isequipped(2782)) {
		mes "������";
		mes "������ �� ����� �� �𢲢�";
		next;
		mes "Eu nao feel confident in conversando desde you nao irium underste anything.";
		close;
	}
	if (ep13_3_invite < 6) {
		mes "Saphum keeps um distance de you ate mesmo when you tentar to conversar. Looks like he chamado on Guardum Galten. Tempo to ir.";
		close;
	}
	if (BaseLevel < 97) {
		mes "[Hunting Dept. 1 Manager]";
		mes "Umm...O que sao you up to? Anything to do com me?";
		close;
	}
	if (checkquest(12159,PLAYTIME) == -1) {
		mes "[Hunting Dept. 1 Manager]";
		mes "Entao you nao pegar";
		mes "any requests yet.";
		mes "Laponte will have piles";
		mes "of requests e might dar";
		mes "you one. Tentar encontrando him.";
		close;
	}
	else if (checkquest(12159,PLAYTIME) == 2) {
		mes "[Hunting Dept. 1 Manager]";
		mes "Tempo limit has expired fou o existing request.";
		next;
		mes "[Hunting Dept. 1 Manager]";
		mes "Se any incomplete request is encontrou, it sera consider esse you falhou it e o request sera deleted.";
		next;
		mes "[Hunting Dept. 1 Manager]";
		mes "Voce pode pegar um novo request depois o existing request record being removed, ir to ver Laponte.";
		que_dic(2,12099,12100,12101,12102,12103,12104,12105,12159);
		close;
	}
	mes "[Hunting Dept. 1 Manager]";
	mes "Bem-vindo.";
	mes "Como pode I ajudar you?";
	next;
	select("I completou o request.");
	mes "[Hunting Dept. 1 Manager]";
	mes "Ah...";
	mes "Voce e done com o request?";
	mes "Deixe-me verificar seu records primeiro.";
	next;
	mes "[Hunting Dept. 1 Manager]";
	set .@s$, "Oh, you completed all your requests. Confirmation complete.";
	que_dic(0,12099,4000,.@s$);
	que_dic(0,12100,49000,.@s$);
	que_dic(0,12101,18000,.@s$);
	que_dic(0,12102,45000,.@s$);
	que_dic(0,12103,4000,.@s$);
	que_dic(0,12104,73000,.@s$);
	que_dic(0,12105,59000,.@s$);
	mes "Um....." + strcharinfo(0) + "";
	mes "Voce vai be in trouble se you lie sobre o request...";
	close;
}

		mes "������";

	next;
	if (countitem(6308) > 29) {

		mes "[Papyrus]";
		mes "Ok aqui's seu 11 Saphum Certifications.";
		delitem 6308,30; //Unidentified_Relic
		getitem 6304,11; //Sapa_Feat_Cert
		close;
	}
	if (countitem(6308) > 2) {
		mes "[Papyrus]";

			close;
		}
		mes "[Papyrus]";
		mes "Ok aqui's seu 1 Saphum Certifications.";
		delitem 6308,3; //Unidentified_Relic
		getitem 6304,1; //Sapa_Feat_Cert
		close;
	}
	mes "[Papyrus]";
	mes "Como pode I ajudar you? ";
	next;
	switch (select("O que e este lugar for? :Como I view documents?:O que e um Unknown Relic? ")) {
		case 1:
			mes "[Papyrus]";
			mes "Este is um importante lugar where datum of various relics collected, desde nosso ancient forefathers, are kept.";

			mes "[Papyrus]";

			mes "Many of us researchers are studying vidum e morte, o birth e extinction of o universe atraves o ancient relics";

	function	eldicastes_dp	{
		if (checkquest(getarg(0)) >= 0) {
			mes "Voce tem encontrou um document you jum have.";
			close;
		}
		mes "^660066"+getarg(2)+"^000000";
		mes "^660066"+getarg(3)+"^000000";
		next;
		mes "Voce tem encontrou um document sobre ^990099"+getarg(1)+"^000000. Verificar fou detalhes in o ^800080Quest Window^000000.";
		specialeffect2 EF_STEAL;
		setquest getarg(0);
		close; 
	}
}

// Unknown Relics
//============================================================
function	script	unknown_d	{
	if (checkquest(getarg(0)) == -1) {
		mes "You ver alguns traces of digging.";
		close;
	}
	specialeffect2 EF_SPRINKLESAND;
	specialeffect2 EF_BEGINSPELL_N1;
	progressbar "ffff00",4;
	erasequest getarg(0);
	mes "You were capaz de encontrar informacao on o "+getitemname(getarg(1))+".";
	specialeffect2 EF_STEAL;
	getitem getarg(1),rand(1,4);
	if (!rand(2)) {
		next;
		mes "In addition, voce tem obtained um unknown relic to relatorio back to o Saphas.";
		specialeffect2 EF_STEAL;
		getitem 6308,1; //Unidentified_Relic
	}
	close;
}

			}

			setarray .@var, 100,200,300,400,500,600,700,800,900;
		}
	}
	else {
		mes .@n$;
		mes "Eu sou done. Hehe";
		close;
	}
	
	// Enchant Constants
	// Ex: .@dex[1]: DEX + 1 | .@cri[5]: CRI + 5 | .@eva[12]: FLEE + 12
	set .@matk[1], 4760; set .@matk[2], 4761;
	set .@eva[6], 4762;	set .@eva[12], 4763;
	set .@cri[5], 4764;	set .@cri[7], 4765;
	set .@atk[2], 4766;	set .@atk[3], 4767;
	set .@dex[1], 4720;	set .@dex[2], 4721;	set .@dex[3], 4722;
	set .@int[1], 4710;	set .@int[2], 4711;	set .@int[3], 4712;	
	set .@agi[1], 4730;	set .@agi[2], 4731;	set .@agi[3], 4732;
	
	// Enchant attribute is determined by the digit position of variable value (ep13_3_<type>).
	// For example, assume the variable value is 516:
	// .@f_<slot>[<index>]
	// slot:	2	3	4
	// index:	5 	1 	6
	
	// Enchant Format
	if (compare(.@v$,"ring")) setarray .@f_2[1],.@agi[1],.@agi[2],.@int[1],.@int[2],.@dex[1],.@dex[2];
	else setarray .@f_2[1],.@agi[1],.@agi[2],.@agi[3],.@int[1],.@int[2],.@int[3],.@dex[1],.@dex[2],.@dex[3];
	setarray .@f_3[1],.@agi[1],.@agi[2],.@int[1],.@int[2],.@dex[1],.@dex[2];
	setarray .@f_4[1],.@atk[2],.@atk[3],.@cri[5],.@cri[7],.@eva[6],.@eva[12],.@matk[1],.@matk[2];
	set .@str$, getd("ep13_3_"+.@v$) + "";
	set .@len, getstrlen(.@str$);
	set .@j, 4;
	set .@c, 1;
	while ((.@len - .@c) > -1 && getd("ep13_3_"+.@v$)) {
		setd ".@e_"+.@j, getd(".@f_"+.@j+"["+atoi(charat(.@str$,.@len-.@c))+"]");
		set .@c, .@c + 1;
		set .@j, .@j - 1;
	}
	for (set .@size, getarraysize(.@rates); .@r <= .@size; set .@r, .@r + 1) {
		if (.@enc_en > .@rates[.@r]) {
			setd ".@e_"+.@j, getd(".@f_"+.@j+"["+.@r+"]");
			break;
		}
	}
	delitem .@Items_,1;
	getitem2 .@Items_,1,1,0,0,.@e_1,.@e_2,.@e_3,.@e_4;
	setd "ep13_3_"+.@v$, .@var[.@r-1] + getd("ep13_3_"+.@v$);
	close;
}

		mes "You nao tenho recebeu um Saphum Certification yet?";
		next;
		mes "[Jahbong]";
		mes "Volte to me umum vez you have one. Eu vou dar you algo bom in exchange.";
		close; 
	}
	set .@cattalkj,rand(1,9);
	if (.@cattalkj == 1) mes "Don't touch my back! I might bite you unconsciously.";
	else if (.@cattalkj == 2) mes "Pub owner Shay carried something away to make something strange! Be careful!";
	else if (.@cattalkj == 3) mes "We cats can jump 5 times our size. Isn't it cool? But then, don't ask me to jump from here now.";
	else if (.@cattalkj == 4) mes "I have to be diligent with my grooming to maintain beautiful fur.";
	else if (.@cattalkj == 5) mes "I have to groom myself everyday to maintain shiny fluffy fur. But I don't have very nice fur in the first place. Very sad.";
	else if (.@cattalkj == 6) mes "You should receive a Sapha Certification with both paws as a sign of respect. No cat deserves a certification if they don't!";
	else if (.@cattalkj == 7) mes "I don't really talk that much! Ahat looks like... meow meow meooow... sorry.";
	else if (.@cattalkj == 8) mes "Meow~~~~~~~~~~";
	else mes "Yarn is a great way to calm a cat.";
	next;
	mes "[Jahbong]";
	mes "Pou que you aqui?";
	mes "Jahbong will exchange ^8000801 Saphum Certification^000000 fou ^8000801 item^000000!";
	next;
	set .@i, select("Apenas came to ver what you have.:Eu quero o Feral Boots!:Me de o Feral Tail!:Me de o Golden Bell!:Quanto to comprar you?")-1;
	switch (.@i) {
		case 0:
			mes "[Jahbong]";
			mes "Meow!";
			next;
			mes "[Jahbong]";
			mes "^8000801Feral Boots^000000 are shoes fou seu feet! Buy one e Jalapeno sobre lum will refine it fou you!";
			next;
			mes "[Jahbong]";
			mes "^8000801Feral Tail^000000 is pretty rough! Mancho sobre lum cum ajudar you consertar it!!";
			next;
			mes "[Jahbong]";
			mes "^8000801Golden Bell^000000 is um accessory. Brare spent 40 years fazendo this! Ou talvez 4 years! Oh bem, volte when voce precisum anything!";
			next;
			mes "[Jahbong]";
			mes "Hum alguns louco aventureiros asking se they cum purchase me! Jahbong is nao fou sale! Eu vou apenas bite you se you ask!!";
			close;
		case 4:
			mes "[Jahbong]";
			mes "Meooooooooooow!!!!!!!!~~ Meooooooooow!!!!!";
			next;
			mes "- ^800080Jahbong goes wild^000000. -";
			next;
			mes "[Jahbong]";
			mes "Eu sou nao um item!! Eu vou nao be merciful se you treat me like one!!";
			next;
			mes "- He may react extremely, mas Jahbong nao parecer to be realmente furioso. -";
			close;
		default:
			break;
	}
	mes "[Jahbong]";
	mes "Voce e realmente comprando algo?!";
	next;
	if (select("Nope:Me de!!!") == 1) {
		mes "[Jahbong]";
		mes "Okay!";
		close;
	}
	setarray .@items[1],2463,2564,2843;
	mes "[Jahbong]";
	mes "Aqui you ir!";
	delitem 6304,1;
	getitem .@items[.@i],1;
	close;
}
